Toggling ON puts the slip in `mode: "betbuilder"` for that match; off reverts to combo/single. |
| BetBuilder slip mode | [`apps/web/src/lib/bet-slip.tsx`](./apps/web/src/lib/bet-slip.tsx) — `mode: "betbuilder"` plus `betbuilderMatchId` + `betbuilderQuote`. While in builder mode `add()` accepts multiple legs from the SAME match (skipping the default same-match replace); picking a leg from a different match drops every builder leg and reverts to combo. |
| BetBuilder rail rendering | [`apps/web/src/components/shell/bet-slip-rail.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/shell/bet-slip-rail.tsx) — re-quotes `/betbuilder/match/:id/quote` whenever the leg set changes, shows the combined session odds, and submits with the `betBuilder` block. |
| BetBuilder settlement payout | [`services/settlement/internal/settler/payout.go`](./services/settlement/internal/settler/payout.go) `BetBuilderPayout` — all-won → `stake × oddsX10000/10_000` (bet_payout); any leg lost → 0; any leg voided + rest non-loss → refund stake (bet_refund) since OBB combined odds aren't a product and we can't recompute on partial voids. |
| OBB proto schema (vendored from `oddin-gg/obbschema`) | [`services/api/proto/obb/`](./services/api/proto/obb/) — `service.proto`, `session.proto`, `markets.proto`, `popular.proto`. Loaded at runtime via `@grpc/proto-loader`; not committed as generated code. |
| RiskZilla engine (TS) | [`services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/engine.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/engine.ts) — runs inside the bet placement transaction, evaluates min stake, max payout, per-tier match liability cap, per-bettor slice (bet_factor × match_cap × RS, ceilinged at the absolute match cap so high RS can't exceed everyone-combined; no automatic damping — operator dials RS down explicitly), bank cap, and per-market factor; on accept bumps `riskzilla_bank_state.open_liability_micro` and writes `riskzilla_event_log`; on reject the placement tx rolls back, then BetsService writes the event_log row outside the rolled-back tx and throws a typed `BadRequestError`. **Combo liability attribution uses NW-share**: each leg's contribution to its (match, market, outcome) bucket is `ticket_payout × (odds_i − 1) / Σ(odds_j − 1)` (model from `HumanDocs/Exhibit 2 - Multibets.xlsx`). A tier-1 match in a combo with a tier-9 partner no longer gets the FULL combo payout charged against its tight cap — only its NW share. Historical bucket sums (other open tickets) are share-weighted on-the-fly via a `leg_shares` CTE so old + new bets stay consistent. Applies to the per-match liability cap gate AND the per-bettor cap gate. **Bank cap is intentionally NOT share-weighted** — it's a hard solvency check against the operator's real crypto cash, and a winning combo pays the full express. `open_liability_micro` (the bank-side counter) tracks the FULL ticket potential payout per ticket for the same reason: if every open ticket wins, the operator owes the sum of full payouts. **Bank gate is the strict version**: `bet_max_loss + open_liability + Σ wallet.balance_micro (USDC) ≤ bank_limit_micro`. Bettor wallet balances are withdrawable on demand and count against the operator's risk capital — accepting a bet that would push committed capital (balances + open potential payouts + this bet) over the bank is rejected with `bank_limit_exceeded` even if the simpler `open_liability + bet ≤ bank` form would otherwise pass. The dashboard + bank pages surface the breakdown as `Bank / Bettor balances / Open liability / Free capacity` with `Free` going red when the operator is over-committed. **`bank_limit_micro` is the operator's REAL crypto-account cash** (seed + Σ credited deposits − Σ confirmed withdrawals + admin manual adjustments) post-migration 0050 — bet outcomes don't move it, only deposits/withdrawals/manual adjustments do. `services/settlement/internal/store/store.go` `UpdateRiskzillaBankOnSettle` / `UpdateRiskzillaBankOnReverse` decrement / re-add `open_liability_micro` on every settle / cancel / rollback (no longer touch `bank_limit_micro`). **Settlement is only one of four exit paths that must release the counter** — `maybeSettleTicket` gates on `status='accepted'`, so it never runs for tickets that left the open set another way. The other three each release inline: the bet-delay worker's `RejectAndRefund` (Go), and — via the shared `releaseOpenLiability()` helper in [`services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/open-liability.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/open-liability.ts) — cashout accept ([`cashout/service.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/cashout/service.ts), `cashed_out`) and admin manual void ([`admin/tickets.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/tickets.ts), `voided`). Forgetting any of these leaks the ticket's full potential payout into the cached counter until `/admin/riskzilla/bank/recompute` rebuilds it from open tickets. **Every writer of `open_liability_micro` must be USDC-gated in BOTH directions** — the engine bypasses OZ entirely, so an unguarded *increment* injects a phantom balance that no decrement can ever remove (the decrements are themselves USDC-gated, and a recompute only sums USDC open tickets). The sixth writer, bet-delay's `AcceptWithUpdatedOdds` re-price delta, was missing that guard until 2026-08-28 and had accumulated 8.5 "USDC" of fictional open liability from OZ re-prices; the counter is CHAR(4)-padded so trim before comparing (`"OZ  "` vs `"USDC"`). Wallet-watcher `CreditIntent` and admin `/admin/deposits/:id/credit-manual` insert `deposit_credit` ledger rows + bump `bank_limit_micro` on credit; admin `/admin/withdrawals/:id/mark-confirmed` inserts a `withdrawal_debit` ledger row + decrements `bank_limit_micro` by `amount` (the `fee` stays in the operator's bank as profit). |
| Alert center (risk desk queue) | [`services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/alert-rules.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/alert-rules.ts) — the rule catalogue, one `INSERT ... SELECT` per kind: `big_stake`, `big_payout`, `high_exposure_ticket`, `sharp_bettor` (company hold ≤ X% over ≥ N settled tickets), `velocity_burst`, `rejection_streak`, `bot_behaviour` (mirrors `bettor_behaviour_scores.alert`), `multi_account_ip` (≥ N bettors on one IP), `flagged_bettor_activity` (labels fraud / suspicious / shady placing bets), `deposit_wrong_token`, `deposit_unattributed`, `withdrawal_stale`, `bank_exposure`, `unsettled_overdue`. Money thresholds are USDC; OZ never alerts. [`alert-sweeper.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/alert-sweeper.ts) runs every 60 s under a Redis NX lock (`ALERT_SWEEPER_DISABLED=1` skips; `POST /admin/riskzilla/alerts/sweep` runs it on demand), upserting by `dedupe_key`. Routes [`admin/riskzilla/alerts.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/riskzilla/alerts.ts): list / summary / detail, `acknowledge` / `assign` / `comment` / `resolve` / `reopen` (each writes `risk_alert_events` + `admin_audit_log` `riskzilla.alert.*`), `GET/PUT alerts/rules/:kind` (audit `riskzilla.alert_rule.update`). UI [`apps/web/src/app/admin/alerts/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/alerts/) (sidebar → Risk & limits → Alert center, badge = active count), the `AlertsBanner` strip on every RiskZilla tab, and an alerts section + chip on the bettor card. Migration `0105`. |
| RiskZilla admin routes (TS) | [`services/api/src/modules/admin/riskzilla/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/riskzilla/routes.ts) — sub-modules `settings`, `bank`, `bettors`, `events`, `dashboard`, `live-delay` (migration 0052 — global / sport / tournament / match cascade override of the live bet acceptance delay; per-leg `match > tournament > sport > global` with MAX across live legs, then MAX with `users.bet_delay_seconds`; **pure-prematch placements get zero delay** — the per-user knob ONLY applies when at least one leg is live; consumed in [`services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts) at placement time). Bank edit (`PUT /admin/riskzilla/bank/limit`) and `/admin/riskzilla/bank/recompute` are extra-gated to a single admin email (`q1qooo@gmail.com`) — anyone else with admin role gets a `bank_admin_only` 403. Recompute rebuilds both `open_liability_micro` (sum of open tickets' potential_payout) AND `bank_limit_micro` from ledger rows (`seed + deposit_credit + withdrawal_debit + manual_adjust where ref_type IS NULL or != 'ticket'` — excludes legacy bet rows and settlement-reverse rows). Every mutation writes `admin_audit_log`. |
| RiskZilla admin pages (web) | [`apps/web/src/app/admin/riskzilla/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/riskzilla/) — `page.tsx` (dashboard KPIs + bank utilisation + top exposure by match + bettor RS histogram), `settings/`, `market-factors/`, `live-delay/` (lazy-loaded sport → tournament → match tree; each row shows its own override or the effective inherited value + source, with per-row Save / Clear; global row seeds at 5s and cannot be deleted), `bank/`, `betticker/` (live tail polling every 3 s with status + reason filter pills + pause; the Status column is the ticket's CURRENT lifecycle state derived in `events.ts`, not the frozen `riskzilla_event_log.decision` — including `rejected_delay` for a bet-delay rejection, which RiskZilla itself recorded as `accepted`, so the generic Rejected filter overlays ticket state too), `bets/` (historical search w/ date-range + cursor pagination), `bettors/` + `bettors/[id]/` (list + per-bettor profile with RS editor). Sidebar entry under **Risk & limits → RiskZilla**. |
| RiskZilla bot controls (placement intent + minimum human time + velocity caps + behaviour scoring) | **Premise (2026-09-03): bet placement cannot be restricted to a physical mouse click** — the server only sees HTTP, Playwright drives a real browser with `isTrusted` clicks, and any "click-only" gate also locks out keyboard and screen-reader users. These controls instead make automation gain nothing and get noticed. **Placement intent** — [`services/api/src/modules/bets/intent.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/intent.ts): `POST /bets/intent` (auth, 120/min) mints a stateless HMAC token (key derived from `JWT_SECRET`) bound to `(user, sorted selection set, issued-at, nonce)`; `POST /bets` requires it when `riskzilla_bot_controls.intent_required` is on (default), rejecting `intent_required` / `intent_invalid` / `intent_selection_mismatch` / `intent_expired` / `intent_too_fast` / `intent_replayed`. The token binds the selection SET, not the odds — binding odds would reset the human-time clock on every live tick and starve a live slip; odds stay validated by the existing authoritative-price + drift path. Single-use is best-effort via a Redis nonce keyed to the placement's idempotency key (a network retry of the same placement passes; Redis down = allow, the signature + TTL + human-time checks still hold). The slip hook [`apps/web/src/lib/use-bet-intent.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/use-bet-intent.ts) quotes on every selection-set change (debounced), refreshes 15 s before expiry, and [`bet-slip-rail.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/shell/bet-slip-rail.tsx) waits out the remaining `min_human_ms` before posting and re-quotes + retries once on a stale token, so bettors never see these codes. **Minimum human time** — `min_human_ms` (default 600) measured server-side from the token's issued-at; a bot can wait, and waiting is exactly what kills the latency-arbitrage edge. Every ticket records `quote_to_place_ms`. **Velocity caps** — `RiskzillaEngine.evaluateVelocity` in [`engine.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/engine.ts), called from `bets/service.ts` for EVERY currency (unlike `evaluate()`, which fences OZ) while the user row is held FOR UPDATE: placements in the trailing 60 s and the union of distinct matches touched, against `cap = max(1, round(base × RS))` from `max_bets_per_minute` / `max_matches_per_minute` (defaults 12 / 10). Rejects as `rejected_velocity` (reason `velocity_bets_per_minute_exceeded` / `velocity_matches_per_minute_exceeded`), logged to `riskzilla_event_log` for USDC and surfaced in the betticker / bets pills. **Behaviour scoring** — pure functions in [`services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/behaviour-score.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/behaviour-score.ts) (unit-tested): per session, `straightness` (chord/path over 6-point windows; interpolated bot moves ≈ 1.0), `speedCv`, `headingEntropy`, `clicksWithoutApproach` (no pointer sample in the 1.5 s before a click — `page.click()` teleports), `clickIntervalCv`; each ramps to a suspicion in [0, 1], the weighted mean is the session score, and fewer than two usable components → NULL (touch devices produce no pointer data; they are left unscored, not flagged). [`behaviour-sweeper.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/behaviour-sweeper.ts) runs every 5 min under a Redis NX lock (`BEHAVIOUR_SWEEPER_DISABLED=1` skips): scores ≤200 settled signed-in `analytics_sessions` with new data, rolls each touched bettor up into `bettor_behaviour_scores` (session scores weighted by sample count over 30 days, blended 80/20 with the share of tickets confirmed within `min_human_ms + 150 ms`), and sets `alert` at `behaviour_alert_threshold` (default 0.7) over ≥ `behaviour_min_sessions` (default 2) with 0.1 hysteresis; a newly raised alert clears any earlier acknowledgement. **Not on the hot path**: placement only ever reads the precomputed row. Config loader [`bot-controls.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/riskzilla/bot-controls.ts) (5 s in-process memo; Postgres, never Redis — see 0095). Admin — [`admin/riskzilla/bot-controls.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/riskzilla/bot-controls.ts) (GET/PUT, audit-logged) + [`admin/riskzilla/behaviour.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/riskzilla/behaviour.ts) (`/admin/riskzilla/behaviour/alerts`, per-bettor `behaviour/acknowledge` + `behaviour/rescore`, audit-logged); `/admin/riskzilla/bettors` gains `botScore` / `botAlert` + a `bot_score` sort, the profile gains a `behaviour` block, the dashboard an `automationAlerts` count. UI — new **Bot controls** tab at [`apps/web/src/app/admin/riskzilla/bot-controls/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/riskzilla/bot-controls/) (config editor + alerts table with Acknowledge / Re-open), a red alert banner + "Automation signals" panel with Rescore on the bettor profile ([`behaviour-panel.tsx`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/riskzilla/bettors/[id]/behaviour-panel.tsx)), a Bot column on the bettors list, and an Automation alerts KPI on the dashboard. Migrations `0096`–`0098`. |
| Catalog scraper friction (per-IP read limits + strict WS Origin) | Cheap layer on the public odds surface (2026-09-03), agreed to stop naive scripts only — anything a browser can fetch a script can fetch, so this is friction, not a wall. **Per-IP rate limits** on the odds-bearing catalog reads via per-route `config.rateLimit` (the api's limiter is `global: false`): `/catalog/sports/:slug`, `/catalog/matches`, `/catalog/matches/:id`, `/catalog/sports/:slug/tournaments`, and the per-match `zillatips` / `zillafacts` / `zillabuild` at 300/min; the polled `/catalog/matches/:id/boosted-odds` (5 s poll) and `/catalog/zillaboost-banners` (10 s) at 600/min; `/catalog/zillaflash` (2 s poll) at 900/min; `/catalog/search` keeps its earlier 60/min. Cheap Redis-cached, odds-free reads (`/catalog/sports`, `/catalog/live-counts`, `/catalog/combi-boost-config`) are deliberately unlimited — every (main) layout render fetches them. **Load-bearing detail:** SSR fetches come from the web replicas, so a per-IP limit would have throttled the whole storefront at ~40 page views/min site-wide. [`apps/web/src/lib/server-fetch.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/server-fetch.ts) therefore forwards the visitor's IP as `X-Forwarded-For` (last inbound entry, shape-checked), the Caddyfile's `web_upstream` block now overwrites `X-Forwarded-For {remote_host}` like the api and ws blocks already did, and Fastify's `trustProxy: 1` takes that value as `request.ip` because the web container is the api's immediate peer. Limits are sized for an office or carrier NAT worth of tabs (a 2 s poll is 30/min per tab). **Strict WS Origin:** `docker-compose.yml` sets `CORS_ORIGINS_STRICT=${CORS_ORIGINS_STRICT:-true}` on ws-gateway, so an upgrade with no Origin header is refused (browsers always send one; nothing server-side opens `/ws`; the Android app is a WebView). Override to `false` in `.env` if a legitimate Origin-less client ever appears. |
| Per-bettor promo visibility (ZillaFlash + CombiBoost + ZillaBuild) | Engine — [`services/api/src/lib/bettor-promo-visibility.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/bettor-promo-visibility.ts) (`loadPromoVisibilityCascades` / `resolveVisible` — one query partitions all rows by `promo_kind`; resolver walks match > tournament > sport > global, returns `true` as the default when no row matches). Cascade rows in `bettor_promo_visibility_config` (migration 0071). Catalog: [`/catalog/zillaflash`](./services/api/src/modules/zillaflash/routes.ts) filters offers per match's cascade resolution when authed; [`/catalog/combi-boost-config`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts) returns `enabled=false` when the user's global combi_boost cascade resolves to false. Placement: [`/bets`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/routes.ts) re-resolves every ZillaFlash leg's (match, tournament, sport) and rejects with `zillaflash_offer_unavailable` if hidden; [`bets/service.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts) strips combi-boost multiplier (sets to 1.0, omits `bet_meta.boostMultiplier`) if ANY leg resolves to hidden — defense in depth so stale clients can't claim a promo the bettor can't see. Admin routes at [`services/api/src/modules/admin/promo-visibility.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/promo-visibility.ts) (summary + tree with all three promo kinds per row + scope CRUD; every mutation audit-logged). Admin UI at [`apps/web/src/app/admin/users/[id]/promo-visibility/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/users/[id]/promo-visibility/) — sport → tournament → match tree with three `On / Off` toggle columns per row (ZillaFlash / CombiBoost / ZillaBuild) + Clear button per kind to drop the override and inherit from parent scope. User-detail page picks up a summary card linking here. |
| ZillaBuild (pre-built BetBuilder cards) | Engine — [`services/api/src/modules/zillabuild/engine.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/zillabuild/engine.ts) (`getZillaBuildForMatch` — OBB `availableMarkets(eventUrn)` → reverse-map to internal markets via `(provider_market_id, canonical(specifiers))` → keep only map-scoped markets in the admin `provider_market_id` allowlist (empty = all) → per (map, slot) KEEP the persisted card if its legs still price, else GENERATE a random 2-4 leg single-map combo validated by OBB `sessionCreate` above the `min_combined_odds` floor → re-quote every read. Prematch-only. Composition persisted in `zillabuild_cards`; combined + per-leg odds are re-quoted from OBB and NEVER stored. Redis cache `zillabuild:v1:{matchId}` (config `cache_ttl_seconds`) + NX gen lock `zillabuild:gen:{matchId}`. Labels via the catalog's `renderOutcomeLabel` / `substituteTemplate` (en). Graceful-idle: OBB disabled/down → empty payload, section hides. Pure combinatorics unit-tested in [`engine.test.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/zillabuild/engine.test.ts)). Catalog route — [`/catalog/matches/:matchId/zillabuild`](./services/api/src/modules/zillabuild/routes.ts) applies the per-bettor `zillabuild` promo-visibility gate (display-only — a card just pre-loads the standard BetBuilder slip, so `/bets` is unchanged). Admin config — [`/admin/zillabuild-config`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/zillabuild-config.ts) (singleton `zillabuild_config`: enabled, eligible `provider_market_id` allowlist, cards-per-map, map-count, legs range, min combined odds, cache TTL; audit-logged). Admin UI — [`apps/web/src/app/admin/zillabuild/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/zillabuild/) (`page.tsx` + `zillabuild-config-editor.tsx`); sidebar entry under **Storefront → ZillaBuild**. Storefront — hook [`apps/web/src/lib/use-zillabuild.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/use-zillabuild.ts) + widget [`apps/web/src/components/match/zillabuild-cards.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/match/zillabuild-cards.tsx) mounted prematch-only on the match page between ZillaFacts and the markets tree; "Use this build" loads the combo into the slip's BetBuilder mode (`setBetbuilderMatch` + `add`). Shared types [`packages/types/src/zillabuild.ts`](./packages/types/src/zillabuild.ts). Migration `0082_zillabuild`. |
| ComboZilla (lobby prebuilt 3-fold carousel) — operator policy | **Which matches may feed the carousel is the operator's call, decided ONCE in the api** (migration `20260906T015446_combozilla_config`, 2026-09-06): [`services/api/src/lib/combozilla.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/combozilla.ts) turns the singleton + rules into one SQL CASE (`comboZillaEligibility`, unit-tested by rendering it through the Postgres dialect so the branch ORDER — tournament > category > sport > tier default — is pinned) and serves two consumers from it: `GET /catalog/combozilla-pool` in [`catalog/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts) (the storefront's candidate set — prematch, bettable, per-bettor adjusted match-winner pair, **capped at 40 per sport** by a window function so football's hundreds of fixtures cannot crowd every other sport out the way the old 60-row `/catalog/matches` window did; flagship tournaments first within a sport; anonymous 3 s cache, busted by every admin write) and the backoffice preview. The pool applies the SAME list predicates as every storefront list — `hasActiveMarket` / `notHiddenTournament` / `notHiddenCategory` moved out of the catalog routes into [`lib/catalog-predicates.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/catalog-predicates.ts) for exactly that reason — so a card can never recommend a match the list below it hides. ZillaBoost is deliberately NOT applied to pool prices: a carousel leg carries no rule id into the slip, so placement would price it from the raw book, and quoting a boost the ticket cannot claim is the one thing this surface must not do. The web builder [`apps/web/src/lib/three-fold-builder.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/three-fold-builder.ts) no longer filters by tier at all — it takes the pool plus `multiCardSportSlugs` and keeps only what is genuinely presentational (odds bands, same-sport legs, the Combi Boost per-leg floor, the per-sport card cap); [`(main)/page.tsx`](./apps/web/src/app/(main)/page.tsx) still drops the bettor's own hidden sports from the pool. Admin — [`services/api/src/modules/admin/combozilla-config.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/combozilla-config.ts) (`GET` returns config + rules hydrated with names + a preview of what the SAVED policy admits grouped sport → tournament with match counts, the UNCAPPED population so the operator sees the real size of what they just allowed; `PUT` config validates slugs against `sports`; `PUT` / `DELETE /rules/:scope/:refId`; every mutation audit-logged) and UI at [`apps/web/src/app/admin/combozilla/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/combozilla/) under **Storefront → ComboZilla**: tier chips T1..T10, an untiered toggle, a multi-card sport picker, an allow / block rule adder that cascades sport → category → tournament off the existing `/admin/tournaments/*` option endpoints, and the preview. Shared wire types [`packages/types/src/combozilla.ts`](./packages/types/src/combozilla.ts) (import by subpath). **Top sports is the other lobby strip operators ask about, and it is already configurable**: pin sports on `/admin/sports` (migration 0103) and they render there in pin order — see the pin-ordering row. |
| Custom Boosted Odds / **ZillaBoost** (operator-facing name) | Banners — [`services/api/src/modules/boosted-odds/banners.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/boosted-odds/banners.ts) (`GET /catalog/zillaboost-banners`, migration 0086) + storefront [`apps/web/src/components/lobby/zillaboost-banners.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/lobby/zillaboost-banners.tsx) (home page: tournament banner / scoreless match card / ZillaFlash-style market card) + sidebar bolt via [`useZillaBoostSportSet`](./apps/web/src/lib/use-zillaboost-banners.ts). Admin board at [`apps/web/src/app/admin/boosted-odds/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/boosted-odds/) is storefront-shaped (sports rail + match cards + tournament chips + Teams tab); the popup sets boost % / end (no end / duration presets / exact time) / Min RS / promo banner. **Selection scope** (migrations `0087`+`0088`): each market row in the expanded match card has an expand caret that lists the market's live selections with their current bettor-facing prices, each individually boostable — the delta comes out of that outcome's own implied probability so its siblings keep their raw price. Engine helpers — [`services/api/src/lib/boosted-odds.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/boosted-odds.ts) (`loadBoostRulesForMatch` / `resolveBoostForMarket` / `loadQuotableOutcomes` / `loadSelectionBoostedMarketIds` / `quoteBoostedMarket`, `passesRiskGate`, and the placement validator `validateCustomBoostForBet`). **All boost pricing goes through ONE shared function** — `quoteMarketBoost` in [`packages/types/src/netwinstable.ts`](./packages/types/src/netwinstable.ts) — called by the match page (realtime, per WS tick), the banners endpoint (server-side per poll), and `POST /bets` (placement re-validation). It owns the fair-book clamp, the per-selection probability-share clamp (`SELECTION_BOOST_MAX_PROB_SHARE`, half — the fair-book clamp alone can't bound a single cell, since a market's headroom can exceed a longshot's whole probability and send its price to infinity), the proportional sharing of scarce headroom across several boosted selections in one market, and the **replacement precedence**: a market carrying any eligible selection rule is priced by its selection rules ALONE (composing them with a market/match-wide key boost would push the book past fair). `formatBoostedOdds` + `isQuotableOutcomeOdds` live in the same module so `boosted-odds.ts` stays free of relative imports — apps/web imports it as a VALUE and the package is NodeNext, so a `.js`-suffixed relative import there passes `tsc` but fails `next build` (see the `oddzilla-types-barrel-imports` footgun). Catalog — [`GET /catalog/matches/:id/boosted-odds`](./services/api/src/modules/boosted-odds/routes.ts) serves RULES only, never prices: per-market entries for market-scope rules, a flat `selections` array for outcome-scope rules (those market ids are stable — the rule pins the row), plus ONE cascade-resolved `matchWide` object (match > competitor > tournament > sport) the client applies to whatever markets it currently renders. Market-scope entries are omitted for markets that have selection rules, and the banner endpoint skips those markets too, so no surface ever advertises a price placement would reject. **Deliberately no market enumeration** — live matches create new ladder-line market rows on odds updates and Oddin suspends the whole book between rounds; the first per-market-flattened version of this endpoint flashed every boost off whenever a poll landed in that window (fixed 2026-08-26). Anonymous-friendly; RS gate treats logged-out viewers as RS 1.000. Outcomes priced at exactly 1.00 stay in the boost computation everywhere (client / placement / banners in parity) so a live favorite ticking through 1.00 can't collapse its market's boost. Placement — [`bets/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/routes.ts) validates per-leg `boostedOddsRuleId` and overwrites `odds` with the authoritative boosted value (±0.01 tolerance, mirrors the ZillaFlash block; a leg carrying both ids keeps the flash offer); [`bets/service.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts) treats it as the server price and stamps `ticket_selections.boost_rule_id`; the bet-delay worker ([`worker.go`](./services/bet-delay/internal/worker/worker.go)) skips per-leg drift for stamped legs. Settlement is untouched — payout reads `odds_at_placement`. Admin — [`services/api/src/modules/admin/boosted-odds.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/boosted-odds.ts) (rules overview + sport → tournament/team → match → market → **selection** tree endpoints + PUT/DELETE rule upsert, audit-logged; `GET /admin/boosted-odds/markets/:marketId/selections` backs the per-market expand row and returns inactive / unpriced outcomes flagged rather than hidden, since Oddin drops individual outcomes inactive for seconds at a time mid-round) and UI at [`apps/web/src/app/admin/boosted-odds/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/boosted-odds/) (green Boost button per row, popup with boost % / end time / Min Risk Score); sidebar entry under **Risk & limits** below ZillaFlash. A market row shows a `N sel` chip when it has boosted selections, and its own market badge greys to `inactive` because the selections have taken over its pricing. **Team boosts (migration 0093):** a competitor rule now picks its market span via `competitor_markets` — `all` (every market of every match the team plays, including the opponent's side and symmetric markets; the original behaviour and the default, so no existing row changed) or `team_only` (just the team's OWN outcome, and only in team-shaped markets). A `team_only` rule is priced through the SELECTION path — the delta comes out of that outcome's own implied probability so the opponent's price never moves — which also means both teams in a match can carry their own team_only boost simultaneously, something the market-wide "higher pct wins" tie-break couldn't express. Team-shaped = `provider_market_id IN (1, 4)` (match winner / map winner, where outcome `1` is the home competitor and `2` the away one); the predicate is [`isTeamShapedMarket`](./packages/types/src/boosted-odds.ts), shared by server and browser rather than copied a third time (ZillaTips' team-of-interest map and the banner endpoint's `teamShaped` flag already keyed off the same two ids). Delivered to the client as `matchWide.teamOutcomeId` — an INSTRUCTION ("boost this outcome wherever the market is team-shaped"), never a market list, because a live match mints new market rows as maps start and an enumerated list would silently miss them. `BatchedMatchBoosts` was collapsed from a `marketWide()` / `selections()` pair into one `resolve(ctx, marketId, providerMarketId)` for the same reason: the split version let a caller take a competitor rule as market-wide by accident. Competitor scope also gains a **banner** (team card linking to `/sport/:slug?team=<id>`, no odds — the rule spans many matches, so there is no single price to quote; the copy says whether it boosts the team's own prices or the whole match). **Fair-odds warning:** the active-rules overview shows a red `!` when the requested boost can't be delivered, because `boostMarketKey` floors its target key at 1.0 and silently cuts the delta on a tight book — an operator would otherwise see a green "+13%" badge while the storefront delivered 7%. Computed exactly in SQL rather than by pricing every market, since the whole clamp reduces to the book key (`bookKey = Σ(1/odds)`): clamped ⇔ `key < 1 + pct/100`, dead ⇔ `key <= 1`, best deliverable pct on a clamped market = `(key − 1) × 100`. Outcome-scope rules are excluded — their binding limit is usually `SELECTION_BOOST_MAX_PROB_SHARE`, so the fair-book number alone would mislead. The algebra is pinned against the real `boostMarketKey` in [`boosted-odds.test.ts`](./packages/types/src/boosted-odds.test.ts) so the two can't drift. **Scheduling (migration 0092):** the popup's **Starts** field (Immediately / Schedule) sets `starts_at`, so a boost can be configured in advance and activate later — nothing renders, prices, or pays out before it. Scheduled rules show grey with `from <time>` in the overview and rail rather than claiming an active boost. One combination is refused client-side: scheduled-start + duration-end, because a duration is measured from SAVE time and would make the rule begin already-expired. Tournaments carry a `T<n>` risk-tier badge (`TierBadge`) in three places — the tournament chip strip, each match card's tournament column, and the active-rules overview — because the tier sets RiskZilla's per-tier match liability cap, so the same boost pct means very different exposure on a tier-1 final than a tier-9 qualifier; a tournament with no Oddin tier renders `T—` rather than omitting the badge, since "unset" is itself information and a missing badge is indistinguishable from a render bug. Storefront **list cards** (lobby / sport page / `/live` / `/upcoming`) price their inline match-winner row SERVER-side — a card has no per-outcome WS subscription, so `/catalog/sports/:slug` + `/catalog/matches` resolve the cascade via the batched [`loadBoostRulesForMatches`](./services/api/src/lib/boosted-odds.ts) (one query covers all six tiers for the whole page) and price it through the same `quoteMarketBoost`. The boost is computed from the RAW published odds and REPLACES the per-bettor adjusted price, mirroring placement exactly (`bets/service.ts` branches on `boostedOddsRuleId` before the adjustment branch, so a boosted leg is priced from raw and skips the adjustment); a suspended outcome stays null so a boost can never resurrect an unbettable cell. **A list card resolves against the market's REAL `provider_market_id`** (fixed 2026-09-07): `quoteMatchWinnerBoost` passed a hard-coded `1` on the premise that the inline row was always Oddin's match winner, which stopped being true when the Fonbet line landed and fails outright on a head-to-head market that carries no `1` or `2` outcome at all — so a `team_only` competitor rule was quoted on Fonbet cards while `validateCustomBoostForBet` refuses it there (`isTeamShapedMarket` reads the real id), and since a typical boost sits inside the 5% drift tolerance the bet was silently accepted at the lower raw price. The card now agrees with placement and the match page. The payload also carries the RESOLVED rule (`boostRule` / `boostSelections`) so [`mergeMatchWithLive`](./apps/web/src/components/match/match-list-tabs.tsx) re-applies the boost over each WS tick instead of reverting to raw — without that the boost flickers off on the first live update (same bug 32286a7 fixed on the match page), and the no-tick fallback must use the pre-boost original or the boost compounds per tick. `MatchRow` carries `boost.ruleId` into the slip: without it placement falls back to the raw price and, since a typical boost sits INSIDE the 5% drift tolerance, the bet is silently accepted at the lower raw price rather than rejected. Storefront **match page** — **prices are realtime**: the match page computes boosted prices client-side through the shared `quoteMarketBoost` over the live outcome set it already tracks via WS ticks, so the boosted price re-derives in the same render as the tick that moved the raw odds; the hook [`apps/web/src/lib/use-boosted-odds.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/use-boosted-odds.ts) polls only the RULES (5 s — admin config propagation + per-viewer Min-RS gate, which is per-user and thus can't ride the shared `odds:match:{id}` channel) and exposes `selectionsByMarket` alongside `byMarket` / `matchWide`, and [`live-markets.tsx`](./apps/web/src/app/(main)/match/[id]/live-markets.tsx) merges the computed entries with ZillaFlash into `AnyBoostEntry` (flash wins on overlap; custom chip shows a countdown ONLY when `ends_at` is set and within an hour). On a selection-boosted market only the boosted cells wear the green styling + struck original price; the header chip still renders once. Slip legs freeze WS price ticks while boosted (active flag still applies) in [`bet-slip-rail.tsx`](./apps/web/src/components/shell/bet-slip-rail.tsx). **The match-scope banner card quotes the MATCH WINNER, three-way, on named rows** (fixed 2026-09-07). Its candidate ranking gave Oddin's `provider_market_id` 1 the top tier and tie-broke the rest by pmid then by the market ROW id — insertion order. A Fonbet football match has no market 1, so every candidate tied at the bottom and insertion order decided: production served Real Madrid vs Inter Milan with **"2nd half: Match result"** and all six of that row's columns (1 / X / 2 / 1X / X2 / 12), labelled by symbol, under a header showing the two team names — so a leg from it reached the slip as "MATCH RESULT / X", indistinguishable from a full-match bet and priced nothing like one. Tier 0 is now [`isMatchWinnerMarket`](./services/api/src/lib/match-winner-market.ts) (see its row above), which is unambiguous on the whole live catalogue. Three details are load-bearing: **(1)** the whole priced book is quoted and only the DISPLAY list is narrowed to `1` / `2` / `3` — dropping outcomes before `quoteBoostedMarket` would change the book key the fair-book clamp reads, and the boost has to come out the same number here, on the match page and in `validateCustomBoostForBet`; **(2)** `teamShaped` keys off the same rule rather than off `pmid IN (1,4)`, which is what turns the labels into home / Draw / away, and the client grew a **Draw row** between the two team rows — with `teamShaped` true it renders prices ON the named rows and hides the labelled block, so before that the draw price was silently dropped from every 1X2 card, Oddin's eFootball included; **(3)** outcomes are sorted `WINNER_OUTCOME_ORDER` (home / draw / away) server-side so the client walks them in render order. Deliberately unchanged: the market-scope card, where the operator pinned that exact market and every outcome of it is the offer. Verified end to end against production data — one market qualifies of the 204 active on that fixture, and the Draw cell lands in the slip as "MATCH RESULT / Draw / 4.66". Shared types [`packages/types/src/boosted-odds.ts`](./packages/types/src/boosted-odds.ts). Migrations `0085_boosted_odds`, `0086_zillaboost_banner`, `0087_boosted_odds_outcome_scope`, `0088_boosted_odds_outcome_rules`. |
| ZillaBoost graphics banners (AI-generated promo art) | Admin popup option **Generate graphics banner** (implies the promo banner; available for every scope that HAS a banner surface — so sport / tournament / competitor / match / market, but not `outcome`, which has none. Competitor became eligible with migration 0093 when team banners shipped; the job context already carried `competitorName` + brand colour, so the prompt path needed no change, and the team banner renders the art as a scrimmed backdrop like the sport and tournament ones) → `boosted_odds_config.graphics_banner` + one queue row in `zillaboost_banner_image_jobs` (migration `0089`; rule_id PK — regenerate resets the SAME row, previous image stays until the replacement lands so the storefront banner never blanks). **Pull model, worker on the OPERATOR PC** (same shape as `services/support-ai-bot` — this is deliberate and security-load-bearing): [`services/zillaboost-banner-gen`](./services/zillaboost-banner-gen/) runs on the machine hosting ComfyUI, polls [`/webhooks/banner-gen/:secret/*`](./services/api/src/modules/boosted-odds/banner-gen.ts) over outbound HTTPS (constant-time token = `BANNER_GEN_TOKEN`, graceful-idle 503 when unset), and talks to ComfyUI + LM Studio on `127.0.0.1`. **ComfyUI must never listen beyond loopback** — it is unauthenticated admin-level code execution (workflow graphs are programs; `--enable-manager` installs arbitrary custom nodes), so the server never gets a network path to it. A server-side compose variant reaching the PC over tailscale existed for a few hours on 2026-08-28 and was reverted the same day; tailscale was purged from the box. The worker probes ComfyUI before claiming and sleeps 1 h between probes while it's down (no attempts burned) — "PC off" = jobs wait in the queue and drain on boot. It POSTs `/heartbeat` every 30 s → Redis `bannergen:worker:online` (TTL 90 s) → the "Image worker online" dot + queue counts on `/admin/boosted-odds`. On the RX 7900 XTX box keep `IMAGE_MODEL=flux1-dev-fp8.safetensors` pinned: SD3.5-fp8 crashes its ROCm kernels (`hipErrorLaunchFailure`), and the FLUX path uses the box-verified graph (EmptyLatentImage, sampler cfg 1.0) with an explicit FluxGuidance node at 2.5 and 28 steps. Per job: Wikipedia research on the boosted entities → LM Studio authors a diffusion prompt (the prompt half accepts **any** OpenAI-compatible endpoint via `LM_STUDIO_BASE_URL` + `LM_STUDIO_API_KEY`, so it can move to a hosted gateway; ComfyUI stays local either way. `max_tokens` is 1200 rather than 400 because reasoning models spend the budget on an internal pass first — measured 969 reasoning + 212 text tokens on GLM-5.3-flash, so 400 would have returned an empty prompt) → **ComfyUI** render (POST `/prompt` with a fixed txt2img graph, poll `/history`, fetch `/view`; 1920×640 plate, ~1.2 MP — the original 1152×384 was under half the pixel budget these models train at, which is where mushy faces and duplicated subjects came from) → composite real crests + names (`compose.ts`, sharp) → downscale to 1536×512 → base64 upload (4 MB cap, png/jpeg/webp; WebP by default). **The game's appearance is ground truth supplied to the prompt, not asked for** — [`game-vocab.ts`](./services/zillaboost-banner-gen/src/game-vocab.ts) maps all 32 active sport slugs to TWO clauses — `scene` (what is in the frame) and `style` (how that title actually looks: a Source 2 frame, Valve painted key art, Riot splash art, a broadcast frame) — both appended deterministically so no completion can drop them. `scene` exists because the local LLM does NOT reliably know these titles and left to itself rendered a DOTA 2 match as tactical-shooter soldiers with melee weapons (2026-08-28); `style` exists because asking for "premium esports promo art" produced the same airbrushed picture for all 32 slugs (2026-08-28). The prompt is also PROSE, not tag soup — FLUX reads sentences (T5), and the SD1.5-era `cinematic, dramatic lighting, depth of field` shape only summons the machine-made look; [`prompt.ts`](./services/zillaboost-banner-gen/src/prompt.ts) keeps a banned-vocabulary list that is both forbidden in the system prompt AND stripped from the completion (`scrubSlop`), because a small local model agrees not to use those words and then uses them anyway. Team identity comes from `competitors.brand_color` / `tournaments.brand_color` carried on the job context: the prompt demands a VERSUS composition built from the two teams' actual hex colours. **Real crests and real team names are COMPOSITED onto the finished plate** ([`compose.ts`](./services/zillaboost-banner-gen/src/compose.ts), sharp) — diffusion at banner scale draws a crest as a smeared blob and a name as mangled pseudo-letters, the loudest AI tell there is, so the prompt still forbids both and the genuine assets go on top: crests fetched from `competitors.logo_url` (Oddin CDN or our own byte-serve, sent ABSOLUTE on the job context), names set in a real font over a bottom scrim with a VS mark. **The overlay is sized against the DISPLAY size, not the file**: the plate is 1536×512 and the card paints it into a ~411×137 strip, so the first cut's perfectly reasonable 25px type arrived as an illegible 6.7px — crests and names are now ~34px / ~13px on screen, which looks oversized when you open the PNG and correct where anyone sees it (the tournament line was dropped for the same reason: 4.3px, and the card prints it in crisp UI type just below). Match / market scope ONLY — sport and tournament plates stay bare because the storefront uses those as backdrops with its own copy laid over them, so baked copy would collide and duplicate. Every part is fail-soft: a crest that 404s is skipped, a font that will not rasterise drops the text layer, and sharp missing entirely (new code unpacked on the GPU box without `pnpm install`) ships the bare plate. Prompt + params are echoed back on upload and shown in the admin panel (migrations 0090/0091). Guardrails in the prompt: no text/numbers/logos/people in the RENDER, and the area the overlay lands on kept simple (bottom third on match/market plates, left third on sport/tournament plates). Claim lease 15 min (crash self-returns); real generation failures back off 1 h/attempt, `failed` after 24 (red chip in the admin overview; untick + re-tick to reset). The chip is a **toggle**: expanding it opens a troubleshooting panel with the rendered image beside the exact prompt (selectable + Copy button) and the render params — `seed` + `checkpoint` make a render reproducible by hand in ComfyUI. The worker also POSTs `/heartbeat` every 30 s on its own timer (fire-and-forget, so it keeps beating through multi-minute renders) → Redis `bannergen:worker:online` (TTL 90 s) → the "Image worker online/offline" dot + queue counts strip above the rules table; offline is a normal state, not an error. **Body limits are load-bearing**: the `/complete` route needs its own `bodyLimit` (8 MiB — the 4 MiB decoded cap is ~5.6 MiB as base64) and Caddy needs the matching `@banner_gen_uploads` carve-out above its 1 MiB default, or uploads 413 AFTER a successful render (hit on the first live one). The worker lives on the GPU box (`DESKTOP-IO524Q2`, `ssh localserver`) at `D:\AI\Oddzilla`, run by the `ZillaboostWorker` scheduled task — **updating it is not `git pull`** (that box has no usable git credentials; use the git-bundle procedure in [`docs/OPERATIONS.md`](./docs/OPERATIONS.md), and confirm the restart took new code via the Redis heartbeat key, not the task state). Verified end-to-end 2026-08-28: a CS2 match regenerated with a true versus composition (crimson left / blue right over bomb-site concrete and smoke), prompt + params stored. On the RX 7900 XTX box `IMAGE_MODEL` must stay pinned to FLUX — `sd3.5_large_fp8_scaled` crashes its ROCm kernels with `hipErrorLaunchFailure` and leaves ComfyUI's GPU context wedged (needs a process restart), while the FLUX path uses the box-verified graph (`EmptyLatentImage`, sampler cfg 1.0, `FluxGuidance` 2.5, 28 steps). Image BYTES live on the job row — NOT on `boosted_odds_config`, whose full-row selects ride hot catalog paths. Byte-serve `GET /catalog/zillaboost-banners/:ruleId/image` (immutable + `?v=<generated-ms>`); the banners payload carries `imageUrl` per scope entry and the storefront renders it as a scrimmed backdrop (sport/tournament strips, light-on-dark content) or a 3:1 top strip (match/market cards), with onError falling back to the plain banner. **The 3:1 strip full-bleeds by cancelling the card's 10px/12px padding with negative margins, and that needs `maxWidth: "none"` on the img** — Tailwind preflight's `img, video { max-width: 100% }` in `@layer base` otherwise clamps the `calc(100% + 24px)` back to 100%, which does not centre the image but dumps the whole 24px on the RIGHT edge as a white gap (measured on production 2026-08-28: 328px card, 302px image, 25px gap). Any future full-bleed image needs the same opt-out. |
| Per-bettor odds adjustment (engine + admin) | Engine — [`services/api/src/lib/bettor-odds-adjustment.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/bettor-odds-adjustment.ts) (`loadBettorAdjustmentCascade` / `resolveBettorAdjustmentBp` / `applyBettorAdjustment` — multiplies raw odds by `(1 + bp/10000)`, clamps to `[ADJUSTED_ODDS_FLOOR=1.001, 1/probability]` (fair-odds ceiling plus a low floor so a negative bp on near-1.0 raw odds can't dip the bettor-facing price below 1.0), returns the same up-to-4dp trimmed-to-≥2dp string formatOdds produces). Mirror Go port in [`services/bet-delay/internal/worker/adjust.go`](./services/bet-delay/internal/worker/adjust.go) so the worker's drift check evaluates against the same adjusted price the slip captured. Cascade rows in `bettor_odds_adjustment_config` (migration 0070). Applied per request inside [`services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/catalog/routes.ts) (sport list / cross-sport list / match-detail / Top market helper), inside the placement tx in [`services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/bets/service.ts), and inside `evaluate()` in [`services/bet-delay/internal/worker/worker.go`](./services/bet-delay/internal/worker/worker.go). Live WS-side application — [`services/ws-gateway/src/bettor-adjustment.ts`](./services/ws-gateway/src/bettor-adjustment.ts) holds the per-user cascade cache; `dispatchOdds` in [`services/ws-gateway/src/server.ts`](./services/ws-gateway/src/server.ts) applies bp per subscriber with a zero-allocation fast path (bp=0) and a per-bp memoisation across subscribers of the same match. Payload extension to carry `sportId` + `tournamentId` lives in [`services/odds-publisher/internal/publisher/publisher.go`](./services/odds-publisher/internal/publisher/publisher.go) (`OutboundPayload`). Invalidation channel `bettor_adjustment_invalidated` — published by admin routes at [`services/api/src/modules/admin/odds-adjustment.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/odds-adjustment.ts) on every mutation (summary + sports / tournaments / matches tree + scope CRUD; every mutation audit-logged). Admin UI at [`apps/web/src/app/admin/users/[id]/odds-adjustment/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/users/[id]/odds-adjustment/) (`page.tsx` + `tree-client.tsx` — sport → tournament → match tree with −1% / −0.5% / +0.5% / +1% stepper buttons; user-detail page picks up a summary card linking here). |
| FE analytics (first-party) | Intake — [`services/api/src/modules/analytics/routes.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/analytics/routes.ts) (`POST /analytics/collect`, anonymous-friendly — the auth plugin's cookie preHandler supplies `request.user` when present; 120/min rate limit, 256 KiB body cap, zod caps from `packages/types/src/analytics.ts`, timestamps clamped to a sane window; session upsert never re-owns a session (`COALESCE(user_id, EXCLUDED.user_id)`), and the denormalised counters are bumped by rows that actually inserted so re-delivered batches are no-ops. Also exports `startAnalyticsRetentionSweeper` — hourly Redis NX-lock sweep, 90 d sessions/events + 14 d mouse batches, `ANALYTICS_SWEEPER_DISABLED=1` to skip). Admin read API — [`services/api/src/modules/admin/analytics.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/analytics.ts) (overview KPIs incl. avg/median session length, sessions/day, popular sections/paths, top clicked elements by precomputed `payload->>'label'`; keyset-paginated sessions list with email/uuid search; per-session journey (events by seq) + lazy mouse-trail fetch per path). Tracker — [`apps/web/src/lib/analytics/tracker.ts`](./apps/web/src/lib/analytics/tracker.ts) (per-tab session in `sessionStorage["oz:analytics:session"]` renewed after 30 min idle; page views on every route change, every click with a compact target descriptor — annotate elements with `data-oz-track` for stable labels; mouse sampling one point / 120 ms while moving; 30 s visibility heartbeats; 5 s fetch flush + sendBeacon/keepalive final flush on pagehide/tab-hide; skips `navigator.webdriver` and `/admin` paths) mounted from the ROOT layout via [`analytics-tracker.tsx`](./apps/web/src/lib/analytics/analytics-tracker.tsx). Admin UI — [`apps/web/src/app/admin/analytics/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/analytics/) (overview + `sessions/[id]` journey timeline + SVG mouse-trail replay with click rings); sidebar entry under **Operations → Analytics**. |
| Unsettled monitor (admin) + operator settlement tools | API [`services/api/src/modules/admin/unsettled.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/unsettled.ts) — `GET /admin/unsettled/{summary,matches,matches/:id/markets,tickets}` — plus [`settlement-tools.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/settlement-tools.ts) (2026-09-06) — `GET/POST/DELETE /admin/unsettled/denylist[/:id]`, `GET /admin/unsettled/denylist/:id/markets`, `GET /admin/unsettled/misses`, `POST /admin/unsettled/markets/:id/void`, `POST /admin/unsettled/matches/:id/void-open`; UI [`apps/web/src/app/admin/unsettled/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/unsettled/) (`page.tsx` + `tabs.tsx`, sub-page `denylist/`), sidebar entry under **Operations** next to Wedged matches. **"Unsettled" = the match is over but the book never went terminal**: `matches.status IN ('closed','cancelled')` while `markets.status NOT IN (-3,-4)`. Only two things ever write a terminal market status — Oddin's `bet_settlement` / `bet_cancel` (services/settlement, from AMQP or the Bifrost backup stream) and Fonbet's results grader (`fonbet-ingester internal/settle`, gated by `FONBET_SETTLE_ENABLED`) — so a market neither reached stays open forever and any ticket on it stays `accepted` with the stake debited. The standing measurement, cause taxonomy and plan are in [`docs/SETTLEMENT_COVERAGE_PLAN.md`](./docs/SETTLEMENT_COVERAGE_PLAN.md) (2026-09-06: 4.28% of the markets on the 09-05 fixtures closed > 1 h, two-thirds of the matches, zero tickets). **Markets are grouped by match, not listed flat** (33 597 market rows across 580 matches when this shipped): the match is the unit an operator acts on, and the per-match drill-down carries the ladder-line rows. The tickets tab separates two cases a status column conflates — a leg waiting on a market that never settled, versus `allLegsResolved` where every leg has a result but the ticket was never paid, which is the stranded case settlement's own `ReconcileStranded` sweep heals. **Operator decisions of 2026-09-06 shape what the page may do:** a played market whose result we do not know is NEVER voided automatically (a refund is a wrong settlement for whoever held the winning side), so there is no auto-void sweeper anywhere — but there IS a **Void** button per market and per match in the drill-down, which publishes the Fonbet grader's own `cancel` message onto `settlement.external` (`provider=admin`) so the void takes services/settlement's apply-once path (market `-4`, selections void, paid tickets reversed), finished matches only, every click an `admin_audit_log` row (`settlement.market_void` / `settlement.match_void_open`). The **Unmatched results** tab lists the Fonbet fixtures the grader could not find in the results feed with what the document listed for the same competition (`fonbet_settlement_misses`). The **Market denylist** sub-page manages `fonbet_market_denylist` — the Fonbet shapes no grader can settle from the data we have (point / game winners in a set, player specials, "Special bets"); fonbet-ingester re-reads it every minute and never creates them again, and the open markets already under each rule stay listed there, deactivated but open, in case the grading is written later. No sidebar badge — with tens of thousands of legacy rows it would be permanently red and stop meaning anything. |

| Custom events (operator-authored offer) + liability trading | Pricing math [`packages/types/src/custom-events.ts`](./packages/types/src/custom-events.ts) (`priceCustomMarket`, `bookKey`, `CUSTOM_PROVIDER_MARKET_ID`; pure + unit-tested in [`custom-events.test.ts`](./packages/types/src/custom-events.test.ts)) — imported by BOTH the api that writes prices and the admin UI that previews them as an operator types, because a preview disagreeing with the save is worse than no preview. Three steps: normalise the hand-entered probabilities (60/30/20 is what a human types, so normalise rather than reject), optionally trade the liability, then put the margin on by **Netwinstable Key Adjustment** — the same Betradar algorithm `netwinstable.ts` already applies for ZillaFlash and ZillaBoost, reached from here by a SELF-REFERENCE (`@oddzilla/types/netwinstable`) because a relative `./netwinstable.js` resolves under `tsc` and then fails at `next build`. **Dividing every fair price by `1 + overround` is the obvious method and the wrong one**, and Betradar's own doc names both failures: it cannot put a key on a short price without driving it under 1.0 (a 1.05 favourite at a 110 key becomes 0.95 — a bet that loses money when it WINS, which this codebase hit and papered over with a floor), and it mangles a lopsided book (their example turns a fair 1.11 / 10.0 into 1.01 / 9.1). Netwinstable instead scales every NET WIN by one factor α, so `odds_i = 1 + α·(fair_i − 1)` with α solved to land the key; because α is positive no price can reach 1.0 at all, and longer prices give up more than proportionally while shorter ones give up less. Measured on the five-way book an operator built (85/12/1/1/1 at a 10% key): proportional quoted the 1% shots at **90.90** and the favourite at **1.06**, Netwinstable quotes **69** and **1.12** — the margin comes off the tail, where the money is not. **Authored prices are quoted on a LADDER whose step widens with the price** (`quoteOnLadder` / `LADDER_BANDS`: 0.01 to 10, then 0.1, 0.5, 1, 5) — floored, never rounded, so a price only ever moves toward the house. A flat hundredth is right near evens and ridiculous in the tail, where it quoted a long shot at `90.90`. Quantisation works in integer multiples of the step because binary floating point otherwise lands a value a hair under its own rung (`7.57 / 0.01` is `756.9999999999999`, which floored to 7.56; `73 * 0.1` is `7.300000000000001`). Feed prices were the exception for exactly one day: they kept four decimals across the whole range because Oddin genuinely quotes a near-certain favorite at 1.003 and rounding that to 1.00 prints a price that does not exist. That argument only ever held near 1.00 — a market shipped to the storefront as `4.7619 / 1.1904` on 2026-09-06 (custom) and a live CS2 map as `5.1410 / 3.6860` the same day (feed), both of which read as a machine leaking its arithmetic — so on 2026-09-07 this same ladder was taken up the feed pipeline too, with the sub-1.01 exemption carried over verbatim. The two ladders are byte-identical, duplicated because both modules are pulled into apps/web as values, and pinned against each other by a sweep in [`odds.test.ts`](./packages/types/src/odds.test.ts); see the "Odds display formatting + the quote ladder" row. The exception is below `LADDER_FLOOR` (1.01), where the ladder's only values are 1.00 (unbettable) and 1.01 (longer than the model said), so those keep four decimals — the case `packages/types/src/odds.ts` exists for, reachable here at a 99.5% probability. Flooring takes slightly MORE margin than requested, so the delivered book key sits just above `1 + overround`; the backoffice shows the real key rather than the asked-for one. API engine [`services/api/src/lib/custom-events/pricing.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/custom-events/pricing.ts) — ONE writer (`repriceMarket`) used by both the backoffice save and the sweeper, so the two can never disagree about what a market costs; also `loadExposureByOutcome`, which attributes a combo's payout by the same **net-win share** RiskZilla's liability caps use (`payout × (odds_i − 1) / Σ(odds_j − 1)`), so the two views of "how exposed are we here" agree and a 3-leg combo is not counted three times (measured on production: 50.53 units attributed vs 83.70 summed naively; all 230 singles resolve to share exactly 1). **USDC only** — OZ is the free demo currency, and letting play money move real prices is the same hazard that fences OZ out of RiskZilla's bank counters. **Liability trading**: exposure share per outcome is blended into the priced probability (`p = (1−k)·base + k·moneyShare`), which RAISES the assigned probability of the side taking money and therefore SHORTENS its price while lengthening the others. That is what "maximise the winnings" means for a bookmaker — not picking winners, but pulling the book level so the margin is earned whatever happens. Both inputs sum to 1 so the blend does too and needs no renormalisation; `maxShiftBp` caps how far one outcome may travel from the operator's view so a single large bet cannot walk a price off a cliff (the clamp breaks the sum, so the renormalisation that follows can carry a value slightly back past its cap — it is a close bound, not an exact one, and is documented as such rather than iterated to a fixed point). Sweeper [`liability-sweeper.ts`](./services/api/src/lib/custom-events/liability-sweeper.ts) (every 20 s, Redis NX lock, `CUSTOM_LIABILITY_DISABLED=1` skips, `CUSTOM_LIABILITY_INTERVAL_SECONDS` tunes). It reprices **every open custom market, not only the liability-traded ones**, and that is what makes the book self-healing: repricing is deterministic, so a market with trading off reprices to exactly what it already holds and writes nothing (`repriceMarket` diffs before it updates, so an unchanged market costs no WAL and publishes no tick). The payoff is that when the pricing RULE changes — the 0.01 ladder did, and left every existing market showing 4.7619 — the whole book converges within one pass instead of waiting for an operator to re-save each market by hand — **deliberately NOT a hook inside bet placement**: placement is the hottest money path in the system and already runs a RiskZilla evaluation, a wallet debit and a ledger write in one transaction, so putting the book's arithmetic on that path would mean a repricing failure could fail a bet. Prices lagging the last bet by seconds costs nothing, because placement re-reads and re-validates the authoritative price anyway. Admin API [`services/api/src/modules/admin/custom-events.ts`](./services/api/src/modules/admin/custom-events.ts) (structure CRUD, events, markets, open/suspend, settle, void; every mutation audit-logged; every write scoped to the Custom sport so it can never rename a feed row the ingester would overwrite anyway). Deletes are refused once bets exist — a ticket leg points at a `(market_id, outcome_id)` pair and settlement, bet history and community tickets all read back through it; settle or void instead. Editing outcome LABELS and probabilities is always allowed, but adding or removing outcomes is refused once bets exist, since a removed outcome leaves a bet that can never settle. Outcome ids are Oddin's canonical `1`/`2`/`3` for 2- and 3-way markets, which is not cosmetic: `loadMatchWinnerOdds` pairs a list card's inline price off exactly those ids, so a two- or three-way custom market shows a price on lobby and sport-page cards for free. Wider markets get `o1..oN` precisely so they do NOT match — a five-way market carrying ids 1..5 would render on cards as a three-way slice of itself. UI [`apps/web/src/app/admin/custom-events/`](./apps/web/src/app/admin/custom-events/) (structure + event list; `[id]/` is the market editor with a live price preview, per-outcome exposure, book key and the settle form). Sidebar entry under **Catalog**. **A custom event covers two shapes, and `custom_event_config` (migration `20260906T200754`) is what lets one feature do both.** `layout='markets'` drops the match-up card and renders the event's markets on the card itself: every list card in this product stacks two sides with a score between them, which is right for a fixture and wrong for a question with answers — "Dima and Nastya to unite again" has no home and away side, so the card read "Will unite again" versus "Will not unite again" as if they were teams and the real market sat one click behind them. `ends_at` is the closing date an outright is defined by, and **the non-obvious half is the catalog gate**: `hasActiveMarket` used to drop any `not_started` match whose kickoff was over six hours old, which is the right reading of broken feed data and the wrong one for an event that opens now and resolves in three months — so it carried a carve-out on this window. **Both the gate and the carve-out were removed on 2026-09-07** (see the "Storefront catalog time-gate" row): the gate hid a wedged match from lists without making it unbettable, and the carve-out had to be repeated in four copies of the gate and reached one. `ends_at` is still what the sweeper enforces. Enforcement is the sweeper, which **suspends** rather than settles: the window shutting says betting is over, not that the result is known, and `POST /bets` already refuses an inactive market, so no separate placement guard exists. Both fields live per EVENT rather than per tournament, because both describe the question rather than where it is filed — an operator will want a head-to-head and a season-long outright under one tournament. **An untiered custom tournament is priced at the STRICTEST tier** (`UNTIERED_RISK_TIER` 10), so a fresh custom event opens under-traded rather than over-exposed — the backoffice surfaces that as a `no tier` chip and links to `/admin/tournaments`, because the tight default is otherwise indistinguishable from a broken stake limit. |
| SQL migrations | [`packages/db/migrations/`](./packages/db/migrations/) — `0000_init`, `0001_odds_history_partitions`, `0002_chain_scanner_state`, `0003_drop_news_articles`, `0004_unclassified_sport`, `0005_merge_duplicate_sports`, `0006_market_descriptions`, `0007_purge_non_mvp_sports` (superseded by 0012), `0008_competitors` (teams as first-class per-sport entities), `0010_odds_config_global_unique`, `0011_feed_messages` (raw AMQP message log keyed to match for the `/admin/logs` panel; uniform 7-day retention since `received_at`, with a periodic backfill that re-resolves `match_id` for rows whose URN now maps — closes the insert/auto-map race that previously left orphan rows hidden), `0012_reactivate_non_bot_sports` (re-enables every non-bot sport after the product opened past the 4-MVP scope), `0013_tournament_risk_tier`, `0014_multi_currency` (composite PK on `wallets`; `currency CHAR(4)` on `wallet_ledger` + `tickets`; OZ demo currency), `0015_cashout` (cashout cascade + probability columns + `cashouts` table), `0016_cashout_acceptance_delay`, `0017_tiple_tippot` (tiple/tippot bet products + per-scope `bet_product_config`), `0018_bet_product_per_leg_margin` (`bet_product_config.margin_bp_per_leg`; effective margin compounds multiplicatively as `(1 + margin_bp) × (1 + margin_bp_per_leg)^N − 1` — same shape a combo's overround takes via its odds product, so Tippot's all-wins multiplier stays below an equivalent combo), `0019_fe_market_display_order` (per-sport storefront ordering of market types — `(sport_id, provider_market_id) → display_order`; consulted by `/catalog/matches/:id`, configured at `/admin/fe-settings/markets-order`), `0020_fe_market_order_scope` (adds `scope` column to `fe_market_display_order` distinguishing `match` / `map` / `top`; admin maintains a separate ordering per scope, and the curated `top` list synthesises a "Top" tab on the match-detail page + an inline Top toggle on match list cards), `0021_competitor_logos` (`competitors.logo_url` + `competitors.brand_color` for storefront team branding; admins manage them at `/admin/competitors`), `0022_match_tv_channels` (`matches.tv_channels jsonb` storing the parsed `<tv_channels>` block from Oddin's fixture endpoint; Twitch / YouTube broadcasters render as a live-stream embed above the markets on the match-detail page, fixture_change `STREAM_URL` (106) now triggers a REST refresh), `0023_settlements_market_id_idx` (plain btree on `settlements(market_id)` so reverse-FK probes by market_id can use an index — the existing `(event_urn, market_id, …)` unique can't because event_urn leads. Without it, the recovery flush's `NOT EXISTS` over 4M settlement rows seq-scanned and ran for over a minute, holding row locks on `markets` that blocked feed-ingester and settlement INSERTs.), `0024_community_profiles` (Phase 10.1 — `users.tickets_public BOOL DEFAULT TRUE` (Decision D1), `users.nickname citext UNIQUE`, `users.bio TEXT`, `users.is_ai BOOL DEFAULT FALSE`. CHECK constraints mirror the API zod cap on bio length (≤280) and the `[A-Za-z0-9_]{3,20}` nickname format. `is_ai` is an internal flag for AI seed accounts in Phase 10.4 and is never serialised by any API endpoint per Decision D2.), `0025_community_tickets` (Phase 10.2 — `community_tickets` denormalised read-projection of publicly-resolved tickets driving the `/community` feed. `UNIQUE (ticket_id)` + `ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE` makes the upsert idempotent under settlement replay; rollback / re-settle generations update the same row. `sport_ids INTEGER[]` (GIN-indexed) carries the per-ticket sport set computed by joining `ticket_selections → markets → matches → tournaments → categories`. Authoritative writer is `services/settlement` (Go) inside `SettleTicket` / `ReverseSettledTicket`; cashout (`services/api`, TS) writes inline; admin `POST /admin/community/backfill` recovers any miss.), `0026_audit_hardening` (security audit PR #130 — 4-eyes on `withdrawals` via `approved_by_user_id` / `submitted_by_user_id` / `confirmed_by_user_id` columns + `withdrawals_distinct_approver_confirmer` CHECK enforcing `confirmed_by ≠ approved_by`; unique partial index on `(network, tx_hash) WHERE tx_hash IS NOT NULL` so a duplicate hash is rejected at DB layer; tamper-evident `admin_audit_log` via `prev_hash` + `row_hash BYTEA` columns and a BEFORE INSERT trigger building a SHA-256 chain over the canonical row payload (advisory-locked so concurrent inserts serialise). Verifier function `admin_audit_chain_check()` returns `(id, ok)` per row — any `ok=false` means the row was tampered with after insert. Migration backfills existing rows so the chain is valid from row 1.), `0027_deposit_reorg_safety` (security audit PR #130 — `deposits.block_hash TEXT` captured at insert time so the wallet-watcher processor can verify the canonical chain still contains the same block before crediting; reorg drops the block → row flips to `status='orphaned'` instead of crediting. Nullable; pre-migration rows skip-verify.), `0028_session_family` (security audit PR #130 — `sessions.family_id UUID NOT NULL` + `parent_session_id UUID` for refresh-token replay detection. Login starts a fresh family; refresh continues an existing one. If a refresh is presented for a session that's ALREADY revoked, we revoke the entire family — canonical detection of stolen-token reuse. Backfill: each existing session is its own family root.), `0029_community_achievements` (Phase 10.4 — `achievement_definitions` (id TEXT PK, title, description, icon lucide-slug, sort_order) + `user_achievements` (`(user_id, achievement_id)` composite PK for idempotent unlocks; cascade on user delete). 5 starter badges seeded: `first_win`, `combo_5`, `odds_20`, `payout_100x`, `streak_10`. Currency-agnostic predicates run after every projection write across Go settle, TS cashout, and admin backfill paths.), `0031_betbuilder` (Oddin BetBuilder same-match combo product — extends the `bet_type` enum with `'betbuilder'`. Session id + frozen combined odds ride in `tickets.bet_meta` jsonb (same convention tiple/tippot use). One typed FK column per scope tier (sport_id / tournament_id / match_id) so ON DELETE CASCADE works for free; partial unique indexes per (scope, ref) pin "at most one row per (user, scope, ref)" as a DB invariant. Cascade at catalog read / placement: `match > tournament > sport > global` (first non-NULL wins). Conservative — we don't sum counters across days or UNION slug sets across days: a user who placed 3 bets one day and 2 another carries forward 3/5, not 5/5. Per-day rows are then deleted.
